   17 | entity neorv32_bus_switch is
   18 |   generic (
   19 |     ROUND_ROBIN_EN   : boolean := false;
   20 |     PORT_A_READ_ONLY : boolean := false;
   21 |     PORT_B_READ_ONLY : boolean := false
   22 |   );
   23 |   port (
   24 |     clk_i   : in  std_ulogic;
   25 |     rstn_i  : in  std_ulogic;
   26 |     a_req_i : in  bus_req_t;
   27 |     a_rsp_o : out bus_rsp_t;
   28 |     b_req_i : in  bus_req_t;
   29 |     b_rsp_o : out bus_rsp_t;
   30 |     x_req_o : out bus_req_t;
   31 |     x_rsp_i : in  bus_rsp_t
   32 |   );
   33 | end neorv32_bus_switch;

   35 | architecture neorv32_bus_switch_rtl of neorv32_bus_switch is
   37 |   type state_t is (S_IDLE, S_BUSY_A, S_BUSY_B);
   38 |   signal state, state_nxt : state_t;
   39 |   signal a_req, b_req, sel, sel_q, stb : std_ulogic;
   40 |   signal locked, locked_nxt : std_ulogic_vector(1 downto 0);
   42 | begin
   46 |   arbiter_sync: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
   47 |   begin
   48 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
   49 |       state  <= S_IDLE;
   50 |       sel_q  <= '0';
   51 |       locked <= "00";
   52 |       a_req  <= '0';
   53 |       b_req  <= '0';
   54 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
   55 |       state  <= state_nxt;
   56 |       sel_q  <= sel;
   57 |       locked <= locked_nxt;
   58 |       if (state = S_BUSY_A) then
   59 |         a_req <= '0';
   60 |       else
   61 |         a_req <= a_req or a_req_i.stb;
   62 |       end if;
   63 |       if (state = S_BUSY_B) then
   64 |         b_req <= '0';
   65 |       else
   66 |         b_req <= b_req or b_req_i.stb;
   67 |       end if;
   68 |     end if;
   69 |   end process arbiter_sync;
   74 |   arbiter_fsm: process(state, locked, sel_q, a_req, b_req, a_req_i, b_req_i, x_rsp_i)
   75 |   begin
   77 |     state_nxt  <= state;
   78 |     locked_nxt <= locked;
   79 |     sel        <= '0';
   80 |     stb        <= '0';
   83 |     case state is
   85 |       when S_BUSY_A =>
   87 |         sel <= '0';
   88 |         if (locked(0) = '1') then
   89 |           stb <= a_req_i.stb;
   90 |           if (a_req_i.lock = '0') then
   91 |             state_nxt <= S_IDLE;
   92 |           end if;
   93 |         elsif (x_rsp_i.ack = '1') then
   94 |           state_nxt <= S_IDLE;
   95 |         end if;
   97 |       when S_BUSY_B =>
   99 |         sel <= '1';
  100 |         if (locked(1) = '1') then
  101 |           stb <= b_req_i.stb;
  102 |           if (b_req_i.lock = '0') then
  103 |             state_nxt <= S_IDLE;
  104 |           end if;
  105 |         elsif (x_rsp_i.ack = '1') then
  106 |           state_nxt <= S_IDLE;
  107 |         end if;
  109 |       when others =>
  111 |         locked_nxt <= b_req_i.lock & a_req_i.lock;
  112 |         if (sel_q = '1') or (not ROUND_ROBIN_EN) then
  113 |           if (a_req_i.stb = '1') or (a_req = '1') then
  114 |             sel       <= '0';
  115 |             stb       <= '1';
  116 |             state_nxt <= S_BUSY_A;
  117 |           elsif (b_req_i.stb = '1') or (b_req = '1') then
  118 |             sel       <= '1';
  119 |             stb       <= '1';
  120 |             state_nxt <= S_BUSY_B;
  121 |           end if;
  122 |         else
  123 |           if (b_req_i.stb = '1') or (b_req = '1') then
  124 |             sel       <= '1';
  125 |             stb       <= '1';
  126 |             state_nxt <= S_BUSY_B;
  127 |           elsif (a_req_i.stb = '1') or (a_req = '1') then
  128 |             sel       <= '0';
  129 |             stb       <= '1';
  130 |             state_nxt <= S_BUSY_A;
  131 |           end if;
  132 |         end if;
  134 |     end case;
  135 |   end process arbiter_fsm;
  140 |   x_req_o.addr  <= a_req_i.addr  when (sel = '0') else b_req_i.addr;
  141 |   x_req_o.amo   <= a_req_i.amo   when (sel = '0') else b_req_i.amo;
  142 |   x_req_o.amoop <= a_req_i.amoop when (sel = '0') else b_req_i.amoop;
  143 |   x_req_o.lock  <= a_req_i.lock  when (sel = '0') else b_req_i.lock;
  144 |   x_req_o.priv  <= a_req_i.priv  when (sel = '0') else b_req_i.priv;
  145 |   x_req_o.debug <= a_req_i.debug when (sel = '0') else b_req_i.debug;
  146 |   x_req_o.src   <= a_req_i.src   when (sel = '0') else b_req_i.src;
  147 |   x_req_o.rw    <= a_req_i.rw    when (sel = '0') else b_req_i.rw;
  148 |   x_req_o.fence <= a_req_i.fence or b_req_i.fence;
  150 |   x_req_o.data  <= b_req_i.data  when PORT_A_READ_ONLY else
  151 |                    a_req_i.data  when PORT_B_READ_ONLY else
  152 |                    a_req_i.data  when (sel = '0')      else b_req_i.data;
  154 |   x_req_o.ben   <= b_req_i.ben   when PORT_A_READ_ONLY else
  155 |                    a_req_i.ben   when PORT_B_READ_ONLY else
  156 |                    a_req_i.ben   when (sel = '0')      else b_req_i.ben;
  158 |   x_req_o.stb   <= stb;
  163 |   a_rsp_o.data <= x_rsp_i.data;
  164 |   a_rsp_o.ack  <= x_rsp_i.ack when (sel = '0') else '0';
  165 |   a_rsp_o.err  <= x_rsp_i.err when (sel = '0') else '0';
  167 |   b_rsp_o.data <= x_rsp_i.data;
  168 |   b_rsp_o.ack  <= x_rsp_i.ack when (sel = '1') else '0';
  169 |   b_rsp_o.err  <= x_rsp_i.err when (sel = '1') else '0';
  172 | end neorv32_bus_switch_rtl;
  185 | library ieee;
  186 | use ieee.std_logic_1164.all;
  188 | library neorv32;
  189 | use neorv32.neorv32_package.all;

  191 | entity neorv32_bus_reg is
  192 |   generic (
  193 |     REQ_REG_EN : boolean := false;
  194 |     RSP_REG_EN : boolean := false
  195 |   );
  196 |   port (
  198 |     clk_i        : in  std_ulogic;
  199 |     rstn_i       : in  std_ulogic;
  201 |     host_req_i   : in  bus_req_t;
  202 |     host_rsp_o   : out bus_rsp_t;
  203 |     device_req_o : out bus_req_t;
  204 |     device_rsp_i : in  bus_rsp_t
  205 |   );
  206 | end neorv32_bus_reg;

  208 | architecture neorv32_bus_reg_rtl of neorv32_bus_reg is
  210 | begin
  214 |   request_reg_enabled:
  215 |   if REQ_REG_EN generate
  216 |     request_reg: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  217 |     begin
  218 |       if (rstn_i = '0') then
  219 |         device_req_o <= req_terminate_c;
  220 |       elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  221 |         if (host_req_i.stb = '1') then
  222 |           device_req_o <= host_req_i;
  223 |         end if;
  224 |         device_req_o.stb   <= host_req_i.stb;
  225 |         device_req_o.lock  <= host_req_i.lock;
  226 |         device_req_o.fence <= host_req_i.fence;
  227 |       end if;
  228 |     end process request_reg;
  229 |   end generate;
  231 |   request_reg_disabled:
  232 |   if not REQ_REG_EN generate
  233 |     device_req_o <= host_req_i;
  234 |   end generate;
  239 |   response_reg_enabled:
  240 |   if RSP_REG_EN generate
  241 |     response_reg: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  242 |     begin
  243 |       if (rstn_i = '0') then
  244 |         host_rsp_o <= rsp_terminate_c;
  245 |       elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  246 |         host_rsp_o <= device_rsp_i;
  247 |       end if;
  248 |     end process response_reg;
  249 |   end generate;
  251 |   response_reg_disabled:
  252 |   if not RSP_REG_EN generate
  253 |     host_rsp_o <= device_rsp_i;
  254 |   end generate;
  257 | end neorv32_bus_reg_rtl;
  277 | library ieee;
  278 | use ieee.std_logic_1164.all;
  279 | use ieee.numeric_std.all;
  281 | library neorv32;
  282 | use neorv32.neorv32_package.all;

  284 | entity neorv32_bus_gateway is
  285 |   generic (
  286 |     TIMEOUT : natural;
  288 |     A_EN    : boolean;
  289 |     A_BASE  : std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);
  290 |     A_SIZE  : natural;
  292 |     B_EN    : boolean;
  293 |     B_BASE  : std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);
  294 |     B_SIZE  : natural;
  296 |     C_EN    : boolean;
  297 |     C_BASE  : std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);
  298 |     C_SIZE  : natural;
  300 |     X_EN    : boolean
  301 |   );
  302 |   port (
  304 |     clk_i   : in  std_ulogic;
  305 |     rstn_i  : in  std_ulogic;
  307 |     req_i   : in  bus_req_t;
  308 |     rsp_o   : out bus_rsp_t;
  310 |     a_req_o : out bus_req_t;
  311 |     a_rsp_i : in  bus_rsp_t;
  312 |     b_req_o : out bus_req_t;
  313 |     b_rsp_i : in  bus_rsp_t;
  314 |     c_req_o : out bus_req_t;
  315 |     c_rsp_i : in  bus_rsp_t;
  316 |     x_req_o : out bus_req_t;
  317 |     x_rsp_i : in  bus_rsp_t
  318 |   );
  319 | end neorv32_bus_gateway;

  321 | architecture neorv32_bus_gateway_rtl of neorv32_bus_gateway is
  324 |   constant a_lo_c : natural := index_size_f(A_SIZE);
  325 |   constant b_lo_c : natural := index_size_f(B_SIZE);
  326 |   constant c_lo_c : natural := index_size_f(C_SIZE);
  327 |   signal port_sel : std_ulogic_vector(3 downto 0);
  330 |   type port_bool_list_t is array (0 to 3) of boolean;
  331 |   constant port_en_list_c : port_bool_list_t := (A_EN, B_EN, C_EN, X_EN);
  334 |   type port_req_t is array (0 to 3) of bus_req_t;
  335 |   type port_rsp_t is array (0 to 3) of bus_rsp_t;
  336 |   signal port_req : port_req_t;
  337 |   signal port_rsp : port_rsp_t;
  340 |   signal int_rsp : bus_rsp_t;
  343 |   type keeper_t is record
  344 |     busy : std_ulogic;
  345 |     lock : std_ulogic;
  346 |     cnt  : std_ulogic_vector(index_size_f(TIMEOUT) downto 0);
  347 |     err  : std_ulogic;
  348 |     halt : std_ulogic;
  349 |   end record;
  350 |   signal keeper : keeper_t;
  352 | begin
  356 |   port_sel(0) <= '1' when A_EN and (req_i.addr(31 downto a_lo_c) = A_BASE(31 downto a_lo_c)) else '0';
  357 |   port_sel(1) <= '1' when B_EN and (req_i.addr(31 downto b_lo_c) = B_BASE(31 downto b_lo_c)) else '0';
  358 |   port_sel(2) <= '1' when C_EN and (req_i.addr(31 downto c_lo_c) = C_BASE(31 downto c_lo_c)) else '0';
  361 |   port_sel(3) <= '1' when X_EN and (port_sel(2 downto 0) = "000") else '0';
  366 |   a_req_o <= port_req(0); port_rsp(0) <= a_rsp_i;
  367 |   b_req_o <= port_req(1); port_rsp(1) <= b_rsp_i;
  368 |   c_req_o <= port_req(2); port_rsp(2) <= c_rsp_i;
  369 |   x_req_o <= port_req(3); port_rsp(3) <= x_rsp_i;
  372 |   request: process(req_i, port_sel)
  373 |   begin
  374 |     for i in 0 to 3 loop
  375 |       port_req(i) <= req_terminate_c;
  376 |       if port_en_list_c(i) then
  377 |         port_req(i) <= req_i;
  378 |         port_req(i).stb <= port_sel(i) and req_i.stb;
  379 |       end if;
  380 |     end loop;
  381 |   end process request;
  384 |   response: process(port_rsp)
  385 |     variable tmp_v : bus_rsp_t;
  386 |   begin
  387 |     tmp_v := rsp_terminate_c;
  388 |     for i in 0 to 3 loop
  389 |       if port_en_list_c(i) then
  390 |         tmp_v.data := tmp_v.data or port_rsp(i).data;
  391 |         tmp_v.ack  := tmp_v.ack  or port_rsp(i).ack;
  392 |         tmp_v.err  := tmp_v.err  or port_rsp(i).err;
  393 |       end if;
  394 |     end loop;
  395 |     int_rsp <= tmp_v;
  396 |   end process response;
  399 |   rsp_o.data <= int_rsp.data;
  400 |   rsp_o.ack  <= int_rsp.ack or keeper.err;
  401 |   rsp_o.err  <= int_rsp.err or keeper.err;
  406 |   bus_monitor: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  407 |   begin
  408 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  409 |       keeper.busy <= '0';
  410 |       keeper.lock <= '0';
  411 |       keeper.cnt  <= (others => '0');
  412 |       keeper.err  <= '0';
  413 |       keeper.halt <= '0';
  414 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  415 |       keeper.err  <= '0';
  416 |       keeper.halt <= port_sel(port_sel'left);
  417 |       if (keeper.busy = '0') then
  418 |         keeper.cnt  <= (others => '0');
  419 |         keeper.busy <= req_i.stb;
  420 |         keeper.lock <= req_i.lock;
  421 |       else
  422 |         keeper.cnt <= std_ulogic_vector(unsigned(keeper.cnt) + 1);
  423 |         if ((keeper.cnt(keeper.cnt'left) = '1') and (keeper.halt = '0')) then
  424 |           keeper.err  <= '1';
  425 |           keeper.busy <= '0';
  426 |         elsif (int_rsp.ack = '1') or ((keeper.lock = '1') and (req_i.lock = '0')) then
  427 |           keeper.busy <= '0';
  428 |         end if;
  429 |       end if;
  430 |     end if;
  431 |   end process bus_monitor;
  434 | end neorv32_bus_gateway_rtl;
  451 | library ieee;
  452 | use ieee.std_logic_1164.all;
  454 | library neorv32;
  455 | use neorv32.neorv32_package.all;

  536 |   );
  537 | end neorv32_bus_io_switch;

  539 | architecture neorv32_bus_io_switch_rtl of neorv32_bus_io_switch is
  542 |   component neorv32_bus_reg
  543 |   generic (
  544 |     REQ_REG_EN : boolean := false;
  545 |     RSP_REG_EN : boolean := false
  546 |   );
  547 |   port (
  549 |     clk_i        : in  std_ulogic;
  550 |     rstn_i       : in  std_ulogic;
  552 |     host_req_i   : in  bus_req_t;
  553 |     host_rsp_o   : out bus_rsp_t;
  554 |     device_req_o : out bus_req_t;
  555 |     device_rsp_i : in  bus_rsp_t
  556 |   );
  557 |   end component;
  560 |   constant num_devs_c : natural := 32;
  563 |   constant addr_lo_c : natural := index_size_f(DEV_SIZE);
  564 |   constant addr_hi_c : natural := (index_size_f(DEV_SIZE) + index_size_f(num_devs_c)) - 1;
  567 |   type dev_en_list_t is array (0 to num_devs_c-1) of boolean;
  568 |   constant dev_en_list_c : dev_en_list_t := (
  569 |     DEV_00_EN, DEV_01_EN, DEV_02_EN, DEV_03_EN, DEV_04_EN, DEV_05_EN, DEV_06_EN, DEV_07_EN,
  570 |     DEV_08_EN, DEV_09_EN, DEV_10_EN, DEV_11_EN, DEV_12_EN, DEV_13_EN, DEV_14_EN, DEV_15_EN,
  571 |     DEV_16_EN, DEV_17_EN, DEV_18_EN, DEV_19_EN, DEV_20_EN, DEV_21_EN, DEV_22_EN, DEV_23_EN,
  572 |     DEV_24_EN, DEV_25_EN, DEV_26_EN, DEV_27_EN, DEV_28_EN, DEV_29_EN, DEV_30_EN, DEV_31_EN
  573 |   );
  576 |   type dev_base_list_t is array (0 to num_devs_c-1) of std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);
  577 |   constant dev_base_list_c : dev_base_list_t := (
  578 |     DEV_00_BASE, DEV_01_BASE, DEV_02_BASE, DEV_03_BASE, DEV_04_BASE, DEV_05_BASE, DEV_06_BASE, DEV_07_BASE,
  579 |     DEV_08_BASE, DEV_09_BASE, DEV_10_BASE, DEV_11_BASE, DEV_12_BASE, DEV_13_BASE, DEV_14_BASE, DEV_15_BASE,
  580 |     DEV_16_BASE, DEV_17_BASE, DEV_18_BASE, DEV_19_BASE, DEV_20_BASE, DEV_21_BASE, DEV_22_BASE, DEV_23_BASE,
  581 |     DEV_24_BASE, DEV_25_BASE, DEV_26_BASE, DEV_27_BASE, DEV_28_BASE, DEV_29_BASE, DEV_30_BASE, DEV_31_BASE
  582 |   );
  585 |   type dev_req_t is array (0 to num_devs_c-1) of bus_req_t;
  586 |   type dev_rsp_t is array (0 to num_devs_c-1) of bus_rsp_t;
  587 |   signal dev_req : dev_req_t;
  588 |   signal dev_rsp : dev_rsp_t;
  591 |   signal main_req : bus_req_t;
  592 |   signal main_rsp : bus_rsp_t;
  594 | begin
  598 |   neorv32_bus_reg_inst: neorv32_bus_reg
  599 |   generic map (
  600 |     REQ_REG_EN => INREG_EN,
  601 |     RSP_REG_EN => OUTREG_EN
  602 |   )
  603 |   port map (
  605 |     clk_i        => clk_i,
  606 |     rstn_i       => rstn_i,
  608 |     host_req_i   => main_req_i,
  609 |     host_rsp_o   => main_rsp_o,
  610 |     device_req_o => main_req,
  611 |     device_rsp_i => main_rsp
  612 |   );
  617 |   dev_00_req_o <= dev_req(0);  dev_rsp(0)  <= dev_00_rsp_i;
  618 |   dev_01_req_o <= dev_req(1);  dev_rsp(1)  <= dev_01_rsp_i;
  619 |   dev_02_req_o <= dev_req(2);  dev_rsp(2)  <= dev_02_rsp_i;
  620 |   dev_03_req_o <= dev_req(3);  dev_rsp(3)  <= dev_03_rsp_i;
  621 |   dev_04_req_o <= dev_req(4);  dev_rsp(4)  <= dev_04_rsp_i;
  622 |   dev_05_req_o <= dev_req(5);  dev_rsp(5)  <= dev_05_rsp_i;
  623 |   dev_06_req_o <= dev_req(6);  dev_rsp(6)  <= dev_06_rsp_i;
  624 |   dev_07_req_o <= dev_req(7);  dev_rsp(7)  <= dev_07_rsp_i;
  625 |   dev_08_req_o <= dev_req(8);  dev_rsp(8)  <= dev_08_rsp_i;
  626 |   dev_09_req_o <= dev_req(9);  dev_rsp(9)  <= dev_09_rsp_i;
  627 |   dev_10_req_o <= dev_req(10); dev_rsp(10) <= dev_10_rsp_i;
  628 |   dev_11_req_o <= dev_req(11); dev_rsp(11) <= dev_11_rsp_i;
  629 |   dev_12_req_o <= dev_req(12); dev_rsp(12) <= dev_12_rsp_i;
  630 |   dev_13_req_o <= dev_req(13); dev_rsp(13) <= dev_13_rsp_i;
  631 |   dev_14_req_o <= dev_req(14); dev_rsp(14) <= dev_14_rsp_i;
  632 |   dev_15_req_o <= dev_req(15); dev_rsp(15) <= dev_15_rsp_i;
  633 |   dev_16_req_o <= dev_req(16); dev_rsp(16) <= dev_16_rsp_i;
  634 |   dev_17_req_o <= dev_req(17); dev_rsp(17) <= dev_17_rsp_i;
  635 |   dev_18_req_o <= dev_req(18); dev_rsp(18) <= dev_18_rsp_i;
  636 |   dev_19_req_o <= dev_req(19); dev_rsp(19) <= dev_19_rsp_i;
  637 |   dev_20_req_o <= dev_req(20); dev_rsp(20) <= dev_20_rsp_i;
  638 |   dev_21_req_o <= dev_req(21); dev_rsp(21) <= dev_21_rsp_i;
  639 |   dev_22_req_o <= dev_req(22); dev_rsp(22) <= dev_22_rsp_i;
  640 |   dev_23_req_o <= dev_req(23); dev_rsp(23) <= dev_23_rsp_i;
  641 |   dev_24_req_o <= dev_req(24); dev_rsp(24) <= dev_24_rsp_i;
  642 |   dev_25_req_o <= dev_req(25); dev_rsp(25) <= dev_25_rsp_i;
  643 |   dev_26_req_o <= dev_req(26); dev_rsp(26) <= dev_26_rsp_i;
  644 |   dev_27_req_o <= dev_req(27); dev_rsp(27) <= dev_27_rsp_i;
  645 |   dev_28_req_o <= dev_req(28); dev_rsp(28) <= dev_28_rsp_i;
  646 |   dev_29_req_o <= dev_req(29); dev_rsp(29) <= dev_29_rsp_i;
  647 |   dev_30_req_o <= dev_req(30); dev_rsp(30) <= dev_30_rsp_i;
  648 |   dev_31_req_o <= dev_req(31); dev_rsp(31) <= dev_31_rsp_i;
  653 |   bus_request_gen:
  654 |   for i in 0 to (num_devs_c-1) generate
  656 |     bus_request_port_enabled:
  657 |     if dev_en_list_c(i) generate
  658 |       bus_request: process(main_req)
  659 |       begin
  660 |         dev_req(i) <= main_req;
  661 |         if (main_req.addr(addr_hi_c downto addr_lo_c) = dev_base_list_c(i)(addr_hi_c downto addr_lo_c)) then
  662 |           dev_req(i).stb <= main_req.stb;
  663 |         else
  664 |           dev_req(i).stb <= '0';
  665 |         end if;
  666 |       end process bus_request;
  667 |     end generate;
  669 |     bus_request_port_disabled:
  670 |     if not dev_en_list_c(i) generate
  671 |       dev_req(i) <= req_terminate_c;
  672 |     end generate;
  674 |   end generate;
  679 |   bus_response: process(dev_rsp)
  680 |     variable tmp_v : bus_rsp_t;
  681 |   begin
  682 |     tmp_v := rsp_terminate_c;
  683 |     for i in 0 to (num_devs_c-1) loop
  684 |       if dev_en_list_c(i) then
  685 |         tmp_v.data := tmp_v.data or dev_rsp(i).data;
  686 |         tmp_v.ack  := tmp_v.ack  or dev_rsp(i).ack;
  687 |         tmp_v.err  := tmp_v.err  or dev_rsp(i).err;
  688 |       end if;
  689 |     end loop;
  690 |     main_rsp <= tmp_v;
  691 |   end process;
  694 | end neorv32_bus_io_switch_rtl;
  709 | library ieee;
  710 | use ieee.std_logic_1164.all;
  711 | use ieee.numeric_std.all;
  713 | library neorv32;
  714 | use neorv32.neorv32_package.all;

  716 | entity neorv32_bus_amo_rmw is
  717 |   port (
  719 |     clk_i      : in  std_ulogic;
  720 |     rstn_i     : in  std_ulogic;
  722 |     core_req_i : in  bus_req_t;
  723 |     core_rsp_o : out bus_rsp_t;
  725 |     sys_req_o  : out bus_req_t;
  726 |     sys_rsp_i  : in  bus_rsp_t
  727 |   );
  728 | end neorv32_bus_amo_rmw;

  730 | architecture neorv32_bus_amo_rmw_rtl of neorv32_bus_amo_rmw is
  733 |   type state_t is (S_IDLE, S_READ_WAIT, S_EXECUTE, S_WRITE, S_WRITE_WAIT);
  734 |   type arbiter_t is record
  735 |     state : state_t;
  736 |     cmd   : std_ulogic_vector(3 downto 0);
  737 |     rdata : std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);
  738 |     wdata : std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);
  739 |   end record;
  740 |   signal arbiter, arbiter_nxt : arbiter_t;
  743 |   signal alu_res : std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);
  746 |   signal cmp_opa  : std_ulogic_vector(32 downto 0);
  747 |   signal cmp_opb  : std_ulogic_vector(32 downto 0);
  748 |   signal cmp_less : std_ulogic;
  749 |   signal cmp_res  : std_ulogic_vector(31 downto 0);
  751 | begin
  755 |   arbiter_sync: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  756 |   begin
  757 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  758 |       arbiter.state <= S_IDLE;
  759 |       arbiter.cmd   <= (others => '0');
  760 |       arbiter.rdata <= (others => '0');
  761 |       arbiter.wdata <= (others => '0');
  762 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  763 |       arbiter <= arbiter_nxt;
  764 |     end if;
  765 |   end process arbiter_sync;
  770 |   arbiter_comb: process(arbiter, core_req_i, sys_rsp_i)
  771 |   begin
  772 |     arbiter_nxt <= arbiter;
  773 |     case arbiter.state is
  775 |       when S_IDLE =>
  777 |         if (core_req_i.stb = '1') and (core_req_i.amo = '1') and (core_req_i.amoop(3 downto 2) /= "10") then
  778 |           arbiter_nxt.cmd   <= core_req_i.amoop;
  779 |           arbiter_nxt.wdata <= core_req_i.data;
  780 |           arbiter_nxt.state <= S_READ_WAIT;
  781 |         end if;
  783 |       when S_READ_WAIT =>
  785 |         arbiter_nxt.rdata <= sys_rsp_i.data;
  786 |         if (sys_rsp_i.ack = '1') then
  787 |           arbiter_nxt.state <= S_EXECUTE;
  788 |         end if;
  790 |       when S_EXECUTE =>
  792 |         arbiter_nxt.state <= S_WRITE;
  794 |       when S_WRITE =>
  796 |         arbiter_nxt.state <= S_WRITE_WAIT;
  798 |       when S_WRITE_WAIT =>
  800 |         if (sys_rsp_i.ack = '1') then
  801 |           arbiter_nxt.state <= S_IDLE;
  802 |         end if;
  804 |       when others =>
  806 |         arbiter_nxt.state <= S_IDLE;
  808 |     end case;
  809 |   end process arbiter_comb;
  812 |   sys_req_o.addr  <= core_req_i.addr;
  813 |   sys_req_o.data  <= alu_res when (arbiter.state = S_WRITE) or (arbiter.state = S_WRITE_WAIT) else core_req_i.data;
  814 |   sys_req_o.ben   <= core_req_i.ben;
  815 |   sys_req_o.stb   <= '1' when (arbiter.state = S_WRITE) else core_req_i.stb;
  816 |   sys_req_o.rw    <= '1' when (arbiter.state = S_WRITE) or (arbiter.state = S_WRITE_WAIT) else core_req_i.rw;
  817 |   sys_req_o.src   <= core_req_i.src;
  818 |   sys_req_o.priv  <= core_req_i.priv;
  819 |   sys_req_o.debug <= core_req_i.debug;
  820 |   sys_req_o.amo   <= core_req_i.amo;
  821 |   sys_req_o.amoop <= core_req_i.amoop;
  822 |   sys_req_o.lock  <= core_req_i.lock;
  823 |   sys_req_o.fence <= core_req_i.fence;
  826 |   core_rsp_o.data <= sys_rsp_i.data when (arbiter.state = S_IDLE) else arbiter.rdata;
  827 |   core_rsp_o.err  <= sys_rsp_i.err  when (arbiter.state = S_IDLE) or (arbiter.state = S_WRITE_WAIT) else '0';
  828 |   core_rsp_o.ack  <= sys_rsp_i.ack  when (arbiter.state = S_IDLE) or (arbiter.state = S_WRITE_WAIT) else '0';
  833 |   amo_alu: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  834 |   begin
  835 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  836 |       alu_res <= (others => '0');
  837 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  838 |       case arbiter.cmd(2 downto 0) is
  839 |         when "000"  => alu_res <= arbiter.wdata;
  840 |         when "001"  => alu_res <= std_ulogic_vector(unsigned(arbiter.rdata) + unsigned(arbiter.wdata));
  841 |         when "010"  => alu_res <= arbiter.rdata xor arbiter.wdata;
  842 |         when "011"  => alu_res <= arbiter.rdata and arbiter.wdata;
  843 |         when "100"  => alu_res <= arbiter.rdata or arbiter.wdata;
  844 |         when others => alu_res <= cmp_res;
  845 |       end case;
  846 |     end if;
  847 |   end process amo_alu;
  850 |   cmp_opa  <= (arbiter.rdata(arbiter.rdata'left) and arbiter.cmd(3)) & arbiter.rdata;
  851 |   cmp_opb  <= (arbiter.wdata(arbiter.wdata'left) and arbiter.cmd(3)) & arbiter.wdata;
  852 |   cmp_less <= '1' when (signed(cmp_opa) < signed(cmp_opb)) else '0';
  853 |   cmp_res  <= cmp_opa(31 downto 0) when ((cmp_less xor arbiter.cmd(0)) = '1') else cmp_opb(31 downto 0);
  856 | end neorv32_bus_amo_rmw_rtl;
  872 | library ieee;
  873 | use ieee.std_logic_1164.all;
  875 | library neorv32;
  876 | use neorv32.neorv32_package.all;

  878 | entity neorv32_bus_amo_rvs is
  879 |   port (
  881 |     clk_i      : in  std_ulogic;
  882 |     rstn_i     : in  std_ulogic;
  884 |     core_req_i : in  bus_req_t;
  885 |     core_rsp_o : out bus_rsp_t;
  887 |     sys_req_o  : out bus_req_t;
  888 |     sys_rsp_i  : in  bus_rsp_t
  889 |   );
  890 | end neorv32_bus_amo_rvs;

  892 | architecture neorv32_bus_amo_rvs_rtl of neorv32_bus_amo_rvs is
  894 |   signal state : std_ulogic_vector(1 downto 0);
  895 |   signal rvso, sc_fail : std_ulogic;
  897 | begin
  901 |   rvs_control: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  902 |   begin
  903 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  904 |       state <= (others => '0');
  905 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  906 |       case state is
  908 |         when "10" =>
  910 |           if (core_req_i.stb = '1') and (core_req_i.rw = '1') then
  911 |             if (rvso = '1') then
  912 |               state <= "11";
  913 |             else
  914 |               state <= "00";
  915 |             end if;
  916 |           end if;
  918 |         when "11" =>
  920 |           if (sys_rsp_i.ack = '1') then
  921 |             state <= "00";
  922 |           end if;
  924 |         when others =>
  926 |           if (core_req_i.stb = '1') and (core_req_i.rw = '0') and (rvso = '1') then
  927 |             state <= "10";
  928 |           end if;
  930 |       end case;
  931 |     end if;
  932 |   end process rvs_control;
  935 |   rvso <= '1' when (core_req_i.amo = '1') and (core_req_i.amoop(3 downto 2) = "10") else '0';
  940 |   bus_request: process(core_req_i, rvso, state)
  941 |   begin
  942 |     sys_req_o <= core_req_i;
  943 |     if (rvso = '1') and (core_req_i.rw = '1') then
  944 |       sys_req_o.stb <= core_req_i.stb and state(1);
  945 |     else
  946 |       sys_req_o.stb <= core_req_i.stb;
  947 |     end if;
  948 |   end process bus_request;
  952 |   sc_result: process(rstn_i, clk_i)
  953 |   begin
  954 |     if (rstn_i = '0') then
  955 |       sc_fail <= '0';
  956 |     elsif rising_edge(clk_i) then
  957 |       sc_fail <= rvso and core_req_i.stb and core_req_i.rw and (not state(1));
  958 |     end if;
  959 |   end process sc_result;
  962 |   core_rsp_o.err  <= sys_rsp_i.err;
  963 |   core_rsp_o.ack  <= sys_rsp_i.ack or sc_fail;
  964 |   core_rsp_o.data <= sys_rsp_i.data(31 downto 1) & (sys_rsp_i.data(0) or sc_fail);
  967 | end neorv32_bus_amo_rvs_rtl;
